Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RELOCATION COMMAND
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the source RNC to inform that resources for the relocation are allocated in the target RNC (in case of intra-system relocation) or in the target system (in case of inter-system relocation). Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RELOCATION DETECT
Content: This message is sent by the target RNC to inform the CN that the relocation execution trigger has been received. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RELOCATION COMPLETE
Content: This message is sent by the target RNC to inform the CN that the relocation is completed. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RELOCATION PREPARATION FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the source RNC if the relocation preparation failed. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RELOCATION FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the target RNC to inform the CN that the requested resource allocation failed. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RELOCATION CANCEL
Content: This message is sent by the source RNC to the CN to cancel an ongoing relocation. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RELOCATION CANCEL ACKNOWLEDGE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the source RNC when the relocation has been cancelled. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > SRNS CONTEXT REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the source RNC to indicate the PS RABs for which context transfer shall be performed. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > SRNS CONTEXT RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the source RNC as a response to SRNS CONTEXT REQUEST message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > SRNS DATA FORWARD COMMAND
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the RNC to trigger the transfer of N-PDUs from the RNC to the CN in intersystem change or in some further cases described in TS 23.060 . Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > FORWARD SRNS CONTEXT
Content: This message is sent either by the source RNC to the CN or by the CN to the target RNC to transfer the SRNS Context. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC and RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > PAGING
Content: This message is sent by the CN to request the UTRAN to page a specific UE. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > COMMON ID
Content: This message is sent by the CN to inform the RNC about the permanent NAS UE identity for a user. It may include additional information. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > CN INVOKE TRACE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to request the RNC to start a trace recording session. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > SECURITY MODE COMMAND
Content: This message is sent by the CN to trigger the integrity and ciphering functions over the radio interface. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > SECURITY MODE COMPLETE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC as a successful response to a SECURITY MODE COMMAND message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > SECURITY MODE REJECT
Content: This message is sent by the RNC as an unsuccessful response to a SECURITY MODE COMMAND message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > LOCATION REPORTING CONTROL
Content: This message is sent by the CN to initiate, modify or stop location reporting from the RNC to the CN. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > LOCATION REPORT
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to the CN with information about the UE location. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > DATA VOLUME REPORT REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the CN to request unsuccessfully transmitted data volumes for specific RABs. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > DATA VOLUME REPORT
Content: This message is sent by the RNC and informs the CN about unsuccessfully transmitted data volumes for requested RABs. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > INITIAL UE MESSAGE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to transfer the radio interface initial layer 3 message to the CN. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > DIRECT TRANSFER
Content: This message is sent by both the CN and the RNC and is used for carrying NAS information over the Iu interface. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN and CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > OVERLOAD
Content: This message is sent by either the CN or the RNC to indicate that the control plane of the node is overloaded. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN and CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RESET
Content: This message is sent by both the CN and the RNC and is used to request that the other node be reset. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN and CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RESET ACKNOWLEDGE
Content: This message is sent by both the CN and the RNC as a response to a RESET message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN and CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > ERROR INDICATION
Content: This message is sent by both the CN and the RNC and is used to indicate that some error has been detected in the node. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN and CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented or connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > CN DEACTIVATE TRACE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to request the RNC to stop a trace session for the indicated trace reference. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection Oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RANAP RELOCATION INFORMATION
Content: This message is part of a special RANAP Relocation Information procedure, and is sent between RNCs during Relocation. Direction: RNC - RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Not applicable.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RESET RESOURCE
Content: This message is sent by either the CN or the RNC. The sending entity informs the receiving entity that it requests the receiving entity to release resources and references associated to the Iu signalling connection identifiers of the message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN and CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless. The maximum number of Iu signalling connection identifiers contained in the RESET RESOURCE message shall not exceed the range bound specified for the maxnoofIuSigConIds (max. no. of Iu signalling connection identifiers) as indicated in the table above.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RESET RESOURCE ACKNOWLEDGE
Content: This message is sent by either the CN or the RNC to inform the RNC or the CN that the RESET RESOURCE message has been received. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN and CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RAB MODIFY REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to the CN to request modification of one or more RABs for the same UE. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > LOCATION RELATED DATA REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the CN either to initiate delivery of dedicated assistance data from the RNC to the UE, or to retrieve deciphering keys for the broadcast assistance data. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > LOCATION RELATED DATA RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC as a successful response to a LOCATION RELATED DATA REQUEST message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > LOCATION RELATED DATA FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to report an unsuccessful response to a LOCATION RELATED DATA REQUEST message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > INFORMATION TRANSFER INDICATION
Content: This message is sent by the CN to transfer information to an RNC. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > INFORMATION TRANSFER CONFIRMATION
Content: This message is sent by the RNC as a successful response to an INFORMATION TRANSFER INDICATION message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > INFORMATION TRANSFER FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC as an unsuccessful response to an INFORMATION TRANSFER INDICATION message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > UE SPECIFIC INFORMATION INDICATION
Content: This message is sent by the CN to inform the RNC about information related to this connection. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > DIRECT INFORMATION TRANSFER
Content: This message is sent by both the RNC and the CN in order to transfer specific information. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN and CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > UPLINK INFORMATION EXCHANGE REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to the CN in order to transfer or request specific information. The nature of the exchange i.e. transfer or request of specific information is indicated within the Information Exchange Type IE. The nature of the information to be transferred is indicated within the Information Transfer Type IE. The nature of the information requested is indicated within the Information Request Type IE. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > UPLINK INFORMATION EXCHANGE RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the RNC as a successful response to the UPLINK INFORMATION EXCHANGE REQUEST message. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > UPLINK INFORMATION EXCHANGE FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the RNC as an unsuccessful response to the UPLINK INFORMATION EXCHANGE REQUEST message. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS SESSION START
Content: This message is sent by the CN to establish a MBMS Iu signalling connection and if needed a MBMS RAB. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS SESSION START RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to report the successful outcome of the request from the MBMS SESSION START message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS SESSION START FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to report the unsuccessful outcome of the request from the MBMS SESSION START message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS SESSION UPDATE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to inform the RNC whenever the RA List of Idle Mode UEs changes compared to one previously sent. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS SESSION UPDATE RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to report the successful outcome of the request from the MBMS SESSION UPDATE message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS SESSION UPDATE FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to report the unsuccessful outcome of the request from the MBMS SESSION UPDATE message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS SESSION STOP
Content: This message is sent by the CN to release a MBMS Iu signalling connection and its associated MBMS RAB. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS SESSION STOP RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to report the outcome of the request from the MBMS SESSION STOP message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS UE LINKING REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the CN to make the RNC aware that a given UE, with existing Iu-ps signalling connection, joined/left one or several Multicast Services. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS UE LINKING RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to report the outcome of the request from the MBMS UE LINKING REQUEST message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS REGISTRATION REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to request the CN to register or de-register the RNC for a certain Multicast Service. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless or connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS REGISTRATION RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the RNC as a successful response to the MBMS REGISTRATION REQUEST message. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless or connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS REGISTRATION FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the RNC as an unsuccessful response to the MBMS REGISTRATION REQUEST message. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless or connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS CN DE-REGISTRATION REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the CN to make the RNC aware that a certain Multicast Service is no longer available. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS CN DE-REGISTRATION RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to the CN as a response to the MBMS CN DE-REGISTRATION REQUEST message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connectionless.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS RAB ESTABLISHMENT INDICATION
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to the CN to inform the CN of the establishment of the MBMS RAB corresponding to the MBMS Iu signalling connection used by this message. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS RAB RELEASE REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to request the CN to release the MBMS RAB. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS RAB RELEASE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to order the RNC to release all MBMS resources related to the Iu connection. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > MBMS RAB RELEASE FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to the RNC as an unsuccessful response to the MBMS RAB RELEASE REQUEST message. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > ENHANCED RELOCATION COMPLETE REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to inform the CN that an enhanced relocation is completed. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > ENHANCED RELOCATION COMPLETE RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to inform the RNC that an enhanced relocation is completed in CN. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > ENHANCED RELOCATION COMPLETE FAILURE
Content: This message is sent by the CN to inform the RNC that a failure has occurred in the CN during the enhanced relocation procedure. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > ENHANCED RELOCATION COMPLETE CONFIRM
Content: This message is sent by the RNC to inform the CN that an enhanced relocation is completed. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RANAP ENHANCED RELOCATION INFORMATION REQUEST
Content: This message is part of a special RANAP Enhanced Relocation Information procedure, and is sent between RNCs during enhanced relocation. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Not applicable.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > RANAP ENHANCED RELOCATION INFORMATION RESPONSE
Content: This message is the response message of a special RANAP Enhanced Relocation Information procedure, and is sent between RNCs during enhanced relocation. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Not applicable.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > SRVCC CS KEYS REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the source RNC to the source SGSN to request security information for SRVCC operation. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ SGSN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > SRVCC CS KEYS RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the source SGSN to the source RNC. It contains information necessary for SRVCC operation. Direction: SGSN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > UE RADIO CAPABILITY MATCH REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the SGSN in order to request indications about compatibility between UE radio capabilities and network configuration. Direction: SGSN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > UE RADIO CAPABILITY MATCH RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the RNC in order to transfer indications about compatibility between UE radio capabilities and network configuration Direction: RNC $ arrow $ SGSN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > UE REGISTRATION QUERY REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the RNC in order to query the CN nodes in the domain whether the UE is served by any of the sharing operators in that domain. Direction: RNC $ arrow $ CN. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > UE REGISTRATION QUERY RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by the CN node in order to convey the result of the query. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > REROUTE NAS REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the SGSN in order to request for a rerouting of the INITIAL UE MESSAGE to a DCN. Direction: SGSN $ arrow $ RNC. Signalling bearer mode: Connection oriented.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > General
Content: Subclause 9.2 presents the RANAP IE definitions in tabular format. The corresponding ASN.1 definition is presented in subclause 9.3. In case there is contradiction between the tabular format in subclause 9.2 and the ASN.1 definition, the ASN.1 shall take precedence, except for the definition of conditions for the presence of conditional elements, where the tabular format shall take precedence. When specifying information elements which are to be represented by bitstrings, if not otherwise specifically stated in the semantics description of the concerned IE or elsewhere, the following principle applies with regards to the ordering of bits: The first bit (leftmost bit) contains the most significant bit (MSB); The last bit (rightmost bit) contains the least significant bit (LSB); When importing bitstrings from other specifications, the first bit of the bitstring contains the first bit of the concerned information;
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Message Type
Content: The Message Type IE uniquely identifies the message being sent. It is mandatory for all messages.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > RAB ID
Content: This element uniquely identifies a radio access bearer for a specific CN domain for a particular UE, which makes the RAB ID unique over one Iu connection. The RAB ID shall remain the same for the duration of the RAB even when the RAB is relocated to another Iu connection. The purpose of the element is to bind data stream from the Non Access Stratum point of view (e.g. bearer of call or PDP context) and radio access bearer in Access Stratum. The value is also used in the RNC to relate Radio Bearers to a RAB. The content of this information element is transferred unchanged from the CN node (i.e. MSC or SGSN) via the RNC to the UE by RANAP messages and RRC messages. For RRC messages refer to TS 25.331 . The element contains binary representation of either the Stream Identifier (SI) for CS domain or the Network Service Access Point Identifier (NSAPI) for PS domain. These identifiers are coded in the RAB ID element in accordance with the coding of the Stream Identifier IE and with the coding of the NSAPI IE in TS 24.008 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > RAB Parameters
Content: The purpose of the RAB parameters IE group and other parameters within the RAB parameters IE group is to indicate all RAB attributes as defined in TS 23.107 for both directions.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Cause
Content: The purpose of the Cause IE is to indicate the reason for a particular event for the RANAP protocol. The meaning of the different cause values is described in the following table. In general, "not supported" cause values indicate that the related capability is missing. On the other hand, "not available" cause values indicate that the related capability is present, but insufficient resources were available to perform the requested action.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > CN Domain Indicator
Content: Indicates the CN domain from which the message originates or to which the message is sent.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Trace Type
Content: Indicates the type of trace information to be recorded. Applicable to GERAN Iu Mode only, not applicable to UTRAN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Trigger ID
Content: Indicates the identity of the entity which initiated the trace. Applicable to GERAN Iu Mode only, not applicable to UTRAN. NOTE: Due to inconsistency in the definition of Trigger ID between TS 25.413 and TS 29.002 , it shall be ensured that the Trigger ID IE is coded with at least the minimum number of required octets.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Trace Reference
Content: Identifies a trace session and is globally unique within one PLMN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > UE Identity
Content: This element identifies the element to be traced i.e. a subscriber or a user equipment.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > OMC ID
Content: A variable length element indicating the destination address of the Operation and Maintenance Center (OMC) to which trace information is to be sent. Applicable to GERAN Iu Mode only, not applicable to UTRAN. NOTE: Due to inconsistency in the definition of OMC ID between TS 25.413 and TS 29.002 , it shall be ensured that the OMC ID IE is coded with at least the minimum number of required octets.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Integrity Protection Information
Content: This element contains the integrity protection information (key and permitted algorithms).
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Encryption Information
Content: This element contains the user data encryption information (key and permitted algorithms) used to control any encryption equipment at the RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Chosen Integrity Protection Algorithm
Content: This element indicates the integrity protection algorithm being used by the RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Chosen Encryption Algorithm
Content: This element indicates the encryption algorithm being used by the RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Request Type
Content: This element indicates the type of location request to be handled by the RNC; the related reported area is either a Service Area or a Geographical Area.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Data Volume Reporting Indication
Content: This information element indicates whether or not the RNC has to calculate the unsuccessfully transmitted NAS data amount for a given RAB and to report the amount of unsuccessfully transmitted NAS data when the RAB is released.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > User Plane Mode
Content: This element indicates the mode of operation of the Iu User plane requested for realising the RAB. The Iu User plane modes are defined in TS 25.415 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > UP Mode Versions
Content: UP mode versions IE is an information element that is sent by CN to RNC. It is a bit string that indicates the versions for the selected Iu UP mode that are required and supported by the CN. The Iu User plane mode versions shall be defined and coded as the "Iu UP Mode versions supported" field defined in TS 25.415 . This reference is applicable for both the transparent mode and the support mode for predefined SDU sizes.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Paging Area ID
Content: This element identifies the area where a PAGING message shall be broadcasted. The Paging area ID is either a Location Area ID or a Routing Area ID.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Non Searching Indication
Content: This parameter allows the RNC not to search Common ID when receiving a PAGING message from the CN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Relocation Type
Content: This information element indicates whether the relocation of SRNS is to be executed with or without involvement of the UE. If the UE is involved then a radio interface handover command shall be sent to the UE to trigger the execution of the relocation. If the UE is not involved then the relocation execution is triggered via Iur.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Source ID
Content: The Source ID IE identifies the source for the relocation of SRNS. The Source ID may be e.g. the source RNC-ID (for UMTS-UMTS relocation and UMTS to LTE relocation) or the SAI of the relocation source (in case of UMTS to GSM relocation).
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Target ID
Content: The Target ID IE identifies the target for the relocation of SRNS. The target ID may be e.g. the target RNC-ID (for UMTS-UMTS relocation) or the Cell Global ID of the relocation target (in case of UMTS to GSM relocation). In case of UMTS to E-UTRAN relocation, the Target ID may be either the eNB-ID or the Corresponding RNC-ID of the relocation target. NOTE: The mapping between the Corresponding RNC-ID and the actual eNB-ID of the relocation target is defined by the network operator and is outside the scope of this specification. Preferably the Target RNC ID used for an eNB contains the LAI and RAC mapped from the GUMMEI of the MME serving the target eNB as specified within TS 23.003 . This avoids configuration of additional identity resolutions and also guarantees that LAIs used for E-UTRAN and UTRAN are mutually exclusive.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > MS Classmark 2
Content: The coding of this element is described in TS 25.331 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > MS Classmark 3
Content: The coding of this element is described in TS 25.331 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Source RNC to Target RNC Transparent Container
Content: The Source RNC to Target RNC Transparent Container IE is an information element that is produced by the source RNC and is transmitted to the target RNC. In inter-system handovers to UTRAN, the IE is transmitted from the external relocation source to the target RNC. This IE is transparent to the CN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Old BSS to New BSS Information
Content: The coding of this element is described in TS 48.008 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Target RNC to Source RNC Transparent Container
Content: The Target RNC to Source RNC Transparent Container IE is an information element that is produced by the target RNC and is transmitted to the source RNC. In inter-system handovers to UTRAN, the IE is transmitted from the target RNC to the external relocation source. This IE is transparent to CN. 9.2.1.30a Source to Target Transparent Container The Source to Target Transparent Container IE is an information element that is provided by the source RAN node to the target RAN node. This IE is transparent to CN. 9.2.1.30b Target to Source Transparent Container The Target to Source Transparent Container IE is an information element that is provided by the target RAN node to the source RAN node. This IE is transparent to CN. 9.2.1.30c TAI This element is used to uniquely identify a Tracking Area as defined in TS 36.413 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > L3 Information
Content: The coding of this element is described in TS 48.008 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Number of Steps
Content: Indicates the number of steps to reduce traffic in overload situation.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > DL N-PDU Sequence Number
Content: This IE indicates the radio interface sequence number (PDCP) TS 25.323 of the next downlink N-PDU (PDCP SDU) that would have been sent to the UE by a source system.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > UL N-PDU Sequence Number
Content: This IE indicates the radio interface sequence number (PDCP) TS 25.323 of the next uplink N-PDU (PDCP SDU) that would have been expected from the UE by a source system.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Criticality Diagnostics
Content: The Criticality Diagnostics IE is sent by the RNC or the CN when parts of a received message have not been comprehended or were missing, or if the message contained logical errors. When applicable, it contains information about which IEs were not comprehended or were missing. For further details on how to use the Criticality Diagnostics IE, see Annex A.2.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Key Status
Content: This IE tells if the keys included in a SECURITY MODE COMMAND message are new or if they have been used previously.

Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Global RNC-ID
Content: The Global RNC-ID is used to globally identify an RNC. 9.2.1.39a Extended RNC-ID The Extended RNC-ID is used to identify an RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Service Handover
Content: This IE tells if intersystem handover to GSM should, should not, or shall not be performed for a given RAB.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Message Structure
Content: The Message Structure IE gives information for each level with assigned criticality in a hierarchical message structure from top level down to the lowest level above the reported level for the occurred error (reported in the Information Element Criticality Diagnostics IE).
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Alternative RAB Parameter Values
Content: The purpose of the Alternative RAB Parameter Values IE is to indicate that: Either RAB QoS negotiation is allowed for certain RAB parameters and, in some cases, to indicate also which alternative values to be used in the negotiation; Or an alternative RAB configuration can be requested by the RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Assigned RAB Parameter Values
Content: The purpose of the Assigned RAB Parameter Values IE is to indicate that RAB QoS negotiation has been performed for certain RAB parameters and which values have been chosen.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Requested RAB Parameter Values
Content: The purpose of Requested RAB Parameter Values IE is to either indicate the RAB parameters for which the included different values are being requested, or indicate that the execution of the alternative RAB configuration is requested.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Global CN-ID
Content: Global CN-ID is used to globally identify a CN node. 9.2.1.46a Vertical Accuracy Code This element includes information about the requested vertical accuracy. 9.2.1.46b Response Time This element includes information about the requested response time. 9.2.1.46c Positioning Priority This element includes information about the requested positioning priority. 9.2.1.46d Client Type This element includes information about the client type.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > New BSS to Old BSS Information
Content: The coding of this element is described in TS 48.008 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Inter-System Information Transparent Container
Content: The Inter-System Information Transparent Container IE is an information element that is produced by the external relocation target system and is transmitted to a source RNC. This IE is transparent to the CN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Cell Load Information
Content: The Cell Load Information IE contains the load information of a specific (serving or target) cell for either the Downlink or the Uplink. If the RNC supports cell load-based inter-system handover, this information shall be understood, when available, as the current traffic load in the target cell if included in a RELOCATION PREPARATION FAILURE message, or the traffic load in the target cell assuming a successful completion of the handover in progress if included in a RELOCATION COMMAND message.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Cell Capacity Class Value
Content: The Cell Capacity Class Value IE is the value that classifies the cell capacity with regards to the other cells. The Cell Capacity Class Value IE only indicates resources that are configured for traffic purposes.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Load Value
Content: The Load Value IE contains the total cell load relative to the maximum planned load. It is defined as the load percentage of the Cell Capacity Class.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > RT Load Value
Content: The RT Load Value IE indicates in percents the ratio of the load generated by Real Time traffic relative to the measured Load Value. Real Time traffic corresponds to the Conversational and Streaming traffic classes.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > NRT Load Information Value
Content: The NRT Load Information Value IE indicates the load situation on the cell for the Non Real-Time traffic. Non Real Time traffic corresponds to the Interactive and Background traffic classes.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Source RNC PDCP context info
Content: The purpose of the Source RNC PDCP context info IE is to transfer RNC PDCP context information from a source RNC to a target RNC during an SRNS relocation. This IE is transparent to CN.

Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > UESBI-Iu
Content: The purpose of the UESBI-Iu IE is to either transfer the UE Specific Behaviour Information as defined in TR 25.994 and TR 25.995 or IMEISV from the CN to the RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Cell Load Information Group
Content: The Cell Load Information Group IE is an information element that is produced by source system BSC and is transmitted to target system RNC via transparent containers. This IE contains the load information of the source cell for either the Downlink or the Uplink or both as well as the source cell identifier the included cell load information corresponds to. If the RNC supports cell load-based inter-system handover, this information shall be understood, when available, as the current traffic load in the indicated source cell prior to the relocation.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Source Cell Identifier
Content: The Source Cell Identifier IE identifies the involved cell of the source system for the relocation of SRNS. The Source Cell Identifier IE may be e.g. source GERAN Source Cell ID (in case of GSM to UMTS relocation) or the UTRAN Source Cell ID (in case of UMTS to GSM relocation).

Content: Indicates the type of information that the RNC requests to transfer.

Content: Indicates the information on a Trace Session activated by Management in the RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Equipments To Be Traced
Content: Indicates the UEs that the RNC has to trace using a list of Equipment Identities or a mask on an Equipment Identity. The IMEI Group is identified by all the IMEI values whose digits 1 to 8 are equal to the corresponding digits of the IMEI IE and whose digits n+9 are equal to the corresponding digits of the IMEI IE if the bit bn of the IMEI Mask IE is equal to 1. The IMEISV Group is identified by all the IMEISV values whose digits 1 to 8 are equal to the corresponding digits of the IMEISV IE and whose digits n+9 are equal to the corresponding digits of the IMEI IE if the bit bn of the IMEISV Mask IE is equal to 1.

Content: Indicates how detailed information should be recorded for this trace session in the RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > List Of Interfaces To Trace
Content: Indicates the interface(s) to be traced by RNC.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > Information Exchange ID

Content: Provides the RNC with the requested information.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > PTP RAB ID
Content: This element uniquely identifies a MBMS PTP radio bearer for a particular UE. The value is used in the RNC to relate MBMS PTP Radio Bearers to a MBMS RAB. The content of this information element is transferred unchanged from the SGSN via the RNC to the UE by RANAP messages and RRC messages. For RRC messages refer to TS 25.331 . The element contains binary representation of the Network Service Access Point Identifier (NSAPI). This identifier is coded in the PTP RAB ID element in accordance with the coding of the NSAPI IE in TS 24.008 .

Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > MSISDN
Content: The purpose of the MSISDN IE (TS 29.060 ) is to indicate the primary MSISDN of the subscriber for the handling of the SIPTO at Iu-PS function, as specified in TS 23.060 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > IRAT Measurement Configuration
Content: The IRAT Measurement Configuration IE contains information for instructing the incoming UE to continue measuring the cells of the source RAT after a successful inter-system handover. It is used by the source RAT to specify the minimum radio quality and the period of time measurements should last for triggering a HO report for unnecessary HO to another RAT. A subset of source RAT frequencies to measure may be specified. The measurement bandwidth of a carrier frequency may also be specified in order to improve the measurement accuracy and reduce measurement time. The IRAT Measurement Configuration IE shall contain at least one of the RSRP or RSRQ thresholds. If only one of the thresholds is present, the target RAT will use the present threshold to compare against the measurement results received from the UE. HO Report should be sent if there is either a single source RAT cell whose measurement results exceed the threshold for the whole measurement duration, or a group of source RAT cells together provide such coverage. The cells that exceed the threshold in the first UE measurement report are included in the HO Report. If both thresholds are present, the received radio measurements must exceed both the RSRP and the RSRQ thresholds in order to satisfy the indicated radio conditions. When the HO Report is sent from RNC at the end of the configured measurement duration, it shall set the HO Report Type IE to "Unnecessary HO to another RAT". If the measurement period expires due to an inter-RAT handover towards LTE executed within the configured measurement duration, the RNC shall set the HO Report Type IE in the HO Report to "Early IRAT Handover". No HO Report shall be sent in case no E-UTRAN cell could be included, or if the indicated period of time is interrupted by an inter-RAT handover to a RAT different than LTE or by an intra-UMTS handover with SRNC relocation.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > MDT Configuration
Content: The purpose of the MDT Configuration IE is to provide configuration information for the MDT function.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > M1 Report
Content: This IE defines the parameters for M1 report, FDD report of UE radio measurements as specified in TS 32.422 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > M2 Report
Content: This IE defines the parameters for a M2 report, TDD report of UE radio measurements as specified in TS 32.422 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > MDT Report parameters
Content: This IE defines the report parameters for MDT periodic reports of measurements M1 and M2 as specified in TS 32.422 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Radio Network Layer Related IEs > RNSAP Relocation Parameters
Content: This IE provides additional information for RNSAP Relocation.

Content: This IE provides RAB specific information for RNSAP Relocation.

Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Transport Network Layer Related IEs > Transport Layer Address

Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > Transport Network Layer Related IEs > Iu Transport Association
Content: This element is used to associate the RAB and the corresponding transport bearer. In PS domain this information element is the GTP Tunnel Endpoint Identifier.

Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > SAI

For this protocol, only a Service Area that is defined to be applicable to both the PS and the CS domains shall be used.

Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > RIM Routing Address
Content: This IE identifies the destination node where the RIM Information needs to be routed by the CN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Selected PLMN Identity
Content: This information element indicates the selected core network operator in shared networks.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Redirection Completed
Content: This IE indicates to RNC that the redirection is completed. Direction: CN $ arrow $ RNC
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Redirection Indication
Content: This IE is used by a CN to request rerouting by the RNC to another CN operator. It is only used in MOCN configuration for network sharing non-supporting UEs.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > TMGI
Content: The TMGI uniquely identifies the MBMS Bearer Service.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > MBMS Session Identity
Content: The MBMS Session Identity identifies the session of a MBMS Bearer Service in UTRAN and is used by the UE to recognise repetitions of a session. This IE is transparent to RAN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > MBMS Bearer Service Type
Content: Indicates the type of the MBMS Bearer Service. 9.2.3.39a MBMS Counting Information Indicates to the RNC whether MBMS Counting procedures can be applied in MBMS Broadcast Mode.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > MBMS Session Duration
Content: This IE defines the duration of the MBMS Session.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > MBMS Service Area
Content: The MBMS Service Area IE consists of a list of one or several MBMS Service Area Identities where each MBMS Service Area Identity is frequency agnostic and can be mapped onto one or more cells.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > RA List of Idle Mode UEs
Content: Indicates the list of RAs where idle-mode UEs interested in a given Multicast Service are.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Delta RA List of Idle Mode UEs
Content: Indicates the list of new RAs where idle-mode UEs interested in a given Multicast Service became or moved to, as well as the list of RAs where there is no interested idle-mode UEs in a given Multicast Service any longer.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > MBMS CN De-Registration
Content: Indicates whether the MBMS Session Stop procedure is a normal Session Stop or a total de-registration for a given MBMS Bearer Service.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > MBMS Registration Request Type
Content: Indicates the type of the MBMS Registration Request.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Requested MBMS IP Multicast Address and APN
Content: Informs the RNC about the requested pairs of IP Multicast Address and APN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Requested Multicast Service List
Content: Informs the RNC about the requested Multicast Service list for a particular UE.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > MBMS Session Repetition Number
Content: Informs the RNC about the repetitions of a particular session of a MBMS Bearer Service.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Time to MBMS Data Transfer
Content: This IE denotes the time occurring between the transmission of the MBMS SESSION START message to the RNS and the actual start of the data transfer. The coding of this element is described in TS 48.018 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Redirect Attempt Flag
Content: This IE indicates that the CN should respond with a Redirection Indication IE or a Redirection Completed IE.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Velocity Estimate
Content: The Velocity Estimate IE is used to describe the UE’s velocity. The reference system is the same as used in TS 23.032 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > RAT Type
Content: Indicates the RAT from which the context request originates.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Requested GANSS Assistance Data
Content: This information element is used for indicating the requested GANSS assistance data. This IE is transparent to CN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Higher bitrates than 16 Mbps flag
Content: This information element indicates whether the UE is capable of handling NAS QoS extensions introduced in Rel-7, or not. See also TS 23.060 .
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > PLMN Identity
Content: This information element is used to uniquely indicate a PLMN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Additional CS/PS coordination information
Content: Informs the RNC about additional information related to CS/PS coordination.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > SGSN Group Identity
Content: This IE identies the DCN within the PLMN.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Barometric Pressure
Content: This information element contains the barometric pressure measurement as reported by the target UE.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Information Element Definitions > NAS Related IEs > Civic Address
Content: This information element contains a location estimate for the target UE expressed as a Civic address.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > General
Content: RANAP ASN.1 definition conforms with ITU-T Rec. X.680 and ITU-T Rec. X.681 . The ASN.1 definition specifies the structure and content of RANAP messages. RANAP messages can contain any IEs specified in the object set definitions for that message without the order or number of occurrence being restricted by ASN.1. However, for this version of the standard, a sending entity shall construct a RANAP message according to the PDU definitions module and with the following additional rules: IEs shall be ordered (in an IE container) in the order they appear in object set definitions.. Object set definitions specify how many times IEs may appear. An IE shall appear exactly once if the presence field in an object has value "mandatory". An IE may appear at most once if the presence field in an object has value "optional" or "conditional". If in a tabular format there is multiplicity specified for an IE (i.e. an IE list) then in the corresponding ASN.1 definition the list definition is separated into two parts. The first part defines an IE container list where the list elements reside. The second part defines list elements. The IE container list appears as an IE of its own. For this version of the standard an IE container list may contain only one kind of list elements. NOTE: ``IE'' means an IE in the object set with an explicit id. If one IE needed to appear more than once in one object set, then the different occurrences have different IE IDs. If a RANAP message that is not constructed as defined above is received, this shall be considered as Abstract Syntax Error, and the message shall be handled as defined for Abstract Syntax Error in subclause 10.3.6. Subclause 9.3 presents the Abstract Syntax of RANAP protocol with ASN.1. In case there is contradiction between the ASN.1 definition in this subclause and the tabular format in subclause 9.1 and 9.2, the ASN.1 shall take precedence, except for the definition of conditions for the presence of conditional elements, where the tabular format shall take precedence.
Title Path: Elements for RANAP Communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > Usage of private message mechanism for non-standard use
Content: The private message mechanism for non-standard use may be used: for special operator- (and/or vendor) specific features considered not to be part of the basic functionality, i.e. the functionality required for a complete and high-quality specification in order to guarantee multivendor interoperability; by vendors for research purposes, e.g. to implement and evaluate new algorithms/features before such features are proposed for standardisation. The private message mechanism shall not be used for basic functionality. Such functionality shall be standardised.
